# GRP-1205  grouperInstaller uses the wrong patch index file

after installing grouper, look at the patch index file: grouperPatchStatus.properties

The API will have UI entries, the UI will not have UI entries, but will have WS entries, and the WS only have API entries (even though the patches are installed correctly).  This will affect patching and upgrading

## Comments

### mchyzer - 2015-09-30T21:22:31.986+0000

this is fixed, the new installer will edit the correct files.  Also see GRP-1206, the installer will fix the index file

### jgasper - 2015-10-01T19:09:42.058+0000

Hi Chris,

Sorry I didn't include enough info. The standalone api archive was downloaded and extracted. Then the patcher run against it. That's what generated the errors. 

I've just kicked off a new (Docker) build which is fully automated so I'll let you know how that goes.

### mchyzer - 2015-10-01T19:31:30.755+0000

### jgasper - 2015-10-01T19:44:00.622+0000

Don't know what to say that script (https://github.com/UniconLabs/grouper-demo-docker/blob/2.2.1/Dockerfile) has been running for months with no issue.  Good news is that the installer worked perfectly on the multiple branches/tags of 2.2.1 that I have.
